The value matches design-spec §4 (\"Exit codes\" table) which\n56\treserves `2` for \"Harness error (manifest invalid, claude binary missing,\n57\tetc.)\".\n58\t\n59\t> **Out of scope for T01.04:** FR-SCH2 eval-id regex enforcement.\n60\t> `validate_eval_id` and `InvalidEvalId` land in T01.05 and are wired\n61\t> into the SuiteLoader orchestrator (T01.07) both pre- and\n62\t> post-parameterize-expansion. `validate_manifest` only enforces\n63\t> shape — including the schema-level `evalIdString` regex that is part\n64\t> of `suite.schema.json` — and never reaches the runtime guard.\n65\t\n66\t## JSON-path rendering\n67\t\n68\t`_format_json_path` projects a `jsonschema.ValidationError.absolute_path`\n69\tinto a `$`-rooted notation:\n70\t\n71\t| Underlying path | Rendered |\n72\t|---|---|\n73\t| (empty — top-level missing required) | `$` |\n74\t| `(\"name\",)` | `$.name` |\n75\t| `(\"evals\", 0, \"id\")` | `$.evals[0].id` |\n76\t| `(\"required_binaries\", 0, \"failure_mode\")` | `$.required_binaries[0].failure_mode` |\n77\t\n78\tThe rendering is deterministic across runs (verified by\n79\t`test_violations_ordering_is_deterministic`) so reporter output stays\n80\tdiff-friendly.\n81\t\n82\t## Acceptance criteria → implementation map\n83\t\n84\t| AC bullet (T01.04) | Implementation site |\n85\t|---|---|\n86\t| `validate_manifest(path)` in `src/superclaude/cli/eval/loader.py` raises `SchemaError` for a fixture manifest missing a required field; No scratch dirs or\n   artifact paths are touched.\n2. **Decode** YAML with `yaml.safe_load`. Decode errors are wrapped in\n   `SchemaError` with path `$`.\n3. **Type-check root** — manifest must decode to a mapping; scalars and\n   lists raise `SchemaError`.\n4. **Validate** against `suite.schema.json` using\n   `jsonschema.Draft202012Validator`. All errors are collected, sorted by\n   `(absolute_path, message)`, and rendered into `$`-rooted JSON paths\n   (e.g. `$.evals[0].id`).\n5. **Project** each `manifest[\"evals\"]` row through `EvalSpec.from_dict`\n   (T01.03 / DM-002).\n\n> No filesystem writes occur in any branch of this function — that is the\n> NFR-SEC1 / FR-SCH1 invariant verified by\n> `test_rejection_does_not_write_to_default_scratch_root`.\n\n## Error → exit-code mapping\n\n| Trigger | Raised | Exit code |\n|---|---|---|\n| Manifest file not found / unreadable | `SchemaError` | 2 |\n| YAML decode failure | `SchemaError` | 2 |\n| Decoded root is not a mapping | `SchemaError` | 2 |\n| `suite.schema.json` validation error (any field, any path) | `SchemaError` | 2 |\n| **Schema-valid manifest** | *(no exception; returns `list[EvalSpec]`)* | n/a |\n\n`SCHEMA_ERROR_EXIT_CODE = 2` is the single source of truth — the CLI\nboundary (`eval doctor`, `eval run`) imports it instead of duplicating\nthe literal. The value matches design-spec §4 (\"Exit codes\" table) which\nreserves `2` for \"Harness error (manifest invalid, claude binary missing,\netc.)\".\n\n> **Out of scope for T01.04:** FR-SCH2 eval-id regex enforcement.\n> `validate_eval_id` and `InvalidEvalId` land in T01.05 and are wired\n> into the SuiteLoader orchestrator (T01.07) both pre- and\n> post-parameterize-expansion. `validate_manifest` only enforces\n> shape — including the schema-level `evalIdString` regex that is part\n> of `suite.schema.json` — and never reaches the runtime guard.\n\n## JSON-path rendering\n\n`_format_json_path` projects a `jsonschema.ValidationError.absolute_path`\ninto a `$`-rooted notation:\n\n| Underlying path | Rendered |\n|---|---|\n| (empty — top-level missing required) | `$` |\n| `(\"name\",)` | `$.name` |\n| `(\"evals\", 0, \"id\")` | `$.evals[0].id` |\n| `(\"required_binaries\", 0, \"failure_mode\")` | `$.required_binaries[0].failure_mode` |\n\nThe rendering is deterministic across runs (verified by\n`test_violations_ordering_is_deterministic`) so reporter output stays\ndiff-friendly.\n\n## Acceptance criteria → implementation map\n\n| AC bullet (T01.04) | Implementation site |\n|---|---|\n| `validate_manifest(path)` in `src/superclaude/cli/eval/loader.py` raises `SchemaError` for a fixture manifest missing a required field; error message names the offending JSON path. | `tests/cli/eval/test_schema_validate.py::test_missing_required_field_raises_schema_error_with_field_name` against `fixtures/missing_name_suite.yaml`. |\n| Valid fixture manifest returns a list of `EvalSpec` instances matching the schema's `evals[]` length. | `test_valid_manifest_length_matches_evals_block` against `fixtures/valid_suite.yaml` (2 evals → 2 `EvalSpec`s). |\n| No filesystem writes occur before validation succeeds (verified by a pytest fixture that snapshots `/tmp/eval-runs` before and after a rejection). | `test_rejection_does_not_write_to_default_scratch_root` snapshots `Path('/tmp/eval-runs')` before and after a `SchemaError`. |\n| `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/D-0004/spec.md` records error → exit-code mapping. | This document (see \"Error → exit-code mapping\" table). |\n\n## Caller contract (downstream consumers)\n\n- **FR-CLI4 `eval doctor` (T01.13)** — invokes `validate_manifest` and maps\n  `SchemaError` to exit `SCHEMA_ERROR_EXIT_CODE` with the rendered\n  violations on stderr.\n- **`eval run` (M2 commands)** — calls `validate_manifest` at the top of\n  the run command before any HomeIsolation / scratch-root setup, so a\n  bad manifest cannot leave a half-created scratch tree on disk.\n- **COMP-002 `SuiteLoader` (T01.07)** — wraps `validate_manifest` and\n  layers `validate_eval_id` + capability resolution + parameterize\n  expansion on top of the parsed `list[EvalSpec]`.\n\n## Notes / deferred work\n\n- The schema already enforces the FR-SCH2 regex on `evalEntry.id` via\n  `evalIdString` (T01.02). `validate_manifest` therefore rejects unsafe\n  static ids at the schema layer too; the runtime `validate_eval_id`\n  guard (T01.05) is still authoritative for post-parameterize-expanded\n  ids (E2 → E2.1, …).\n- `SchemaError.violations` is an ordered tuple of `(path, message)` pairs\n  so reporters can render every violation rather than just the first.\n  This matches the FR-SCH1 spirit of \"fail closed with full context.\"\n- `Path.read_text` is used directly (no `open()` context manager) to make\n  the FR-SCH1 invariant (\"no filesystem writes before validation\n  succeeds\") obvious — every IO call in this module is a pure read.\n","numLines":116,"startLine":1,"totalLines":116}}}

The function accepts\n  any value at runtime so non-string scalars (`int`, `bool`, `None`,\n  `bytes`) that may leak through YAML decoding are rejected via the\n  type guard rather than slipping past the regex check.\n- **Output:** `None` on success (the function is a pure guard).\n- **Error behaviour:** raises `InvalidEvalId` carrying the offending\n  value on `InvalidEvalId.eval_id`. CLI callers MUST map this to\n  `INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE` (= 2).\n\n## The regex\n\n```python\nEVAL_ID_REGEX = re.compile(r\"^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?)?$\")\n```\n\nThis is the single source of truth for the runtime guard. The same\nregex literal is encoded into `suites/suite.schema.json` (T01.02) as\nthe `evalIdString` `pattern`, so schema validation rejects unsafe\n**static** ids at the schema layer too; `validate_eval_id` is the\nauthoritative runtime check the security model relies on (especially\nfor post-parameterize-expanded ids that never touch the schema).\n\nThe pattern accepts:\n\n| Shape           | Examples                          |\n|-----------------|-----------------------------------|\n| Canonical eval  | `E1`, `E2`, `E15`, `D15`          |\n| Parameter-ized  | `E2.1`, `E2.2`, `E2.10`, `Foo42.7`|\n| Single letter   | `A`, `Z9`                         |\n| Mixed-case body | `Test1`, `ABC123`                 |\n\nIt rejects everything else; the named negative cases below are the\nfloor, not the ceiling.\n\n## Negative-case checklist (FR-SCH2 / NFR-SEC1)\n\n| Category                  | Examples                                                 | Why it must fail                                              |\n|---------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Empty string              | `\"\"`                                                     | AC bullet; trivially escapes `home_root / \"\" / \"home\"`        |\n| Path-traversal prefix     | `../home`, `../../etc`, `..`                             | AC bullet; escapes the scratch root via `Path` join semantics |\n| Absolute path             | `/etc`, `/tmp/eval-runs`                                 | AC bullet; `Path(\"/abs\") / \"home\"` discards `home_root`       |\n| Embedded separator        | `foo/bar`, `foo\\\\bar`, `E1/x`, `./foo`, `.`              | Lets attacker carve sub-directories under `home_root`         |\n| Leading-digit id          | `1E`, `9`, `0E`, `1`, `12.3`                             | AC bullet; the schema also bans this — runtime mirrors        |\n| Template token leakage    | `{{prefix}}`, `E{{p}}`, `${var}`, `<id>`, `%name%`       | AC bullet; an un-substituted token must NEVER reach the FS    |\n| Whitespace / control char | ` E1`, `E1 `, `E 1`, `\\tE1`, `E1\\n`, `E1\\x00`            | Defence in depth — control chars confuse downstream consumers |\n| Wrong character class     | `e1`, `eval1`, `E-1`, `E_1`                              | Underscores and lowercase are excluded by the regex anchor    |\n| Malformed decimal         | `E1.`, `E.1`, `E1..1`, `E1.1.1`, `E1.1.`                 | Only one `.N` decimal allowed (mirrors parameterize semantics)|\n| Non-string scalar         | `None`, `1`, `1.0`, `b\"E1\"`, `[\"E1\"]`, `(\"E1\",)`, `{...}`| YAML may decode bare scalars; type guard runs before regex    |\n\n> All entries above are exercised by `tests/cli/eval/test_eval_id_regex.py`\n> (T01.05 unit surface, 66 assertions). The dedicated path-traversal\n> negative-case test set (T01.08 / NFR-SEC1) cross-links the same\n> categories at the integration layer.\n\n## Error → exit-code mapping\n\n| Trigger                                      | Raised           | Exit code                  |\n|----------------------------------------------|------------------|----------------------------|\n| `eval_id` is not `str`                       | `InvalidEvalId`  | `INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE`|\n| `eval_id` does not match `EVAL_ID_REGEX`     | `InvalidEvalId`  | `INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE`|\n\n`INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE = 2` is kept as its own constant (even though\nthe value equals `SCHEMA_ERROR_EXIT_CODE`) so call sites can branch on\nthe *intent* of the rejection without coupling the two failure classes\ntogether. Both surface to operators as the same \"harness rejected the\nmanifest before any filesystem write\" outcome (design-spec §4).\n\n## Application sites (FR-SCH2 ordering)\n\n`validate_eval_id` is invoked in two places by the SuiteLoader\norchestrator (T01.07 / COMP-002):\n\n1. **At loader entry**, against every static `evals[].id` in the parsed\n   manifest. This is redundant with the schema layer for well-formed\n   manifests but the security model treats the runtime guard as\n   authoritative.\n2. **After parameterize expansion**, against every generated id\n   (`E2.1`, `E2.2`, ...). This is the *load-bearing* call — it closes\n   the path-traversal attack surface where a malicious parameterize\n   row could otherwise inject `..` or `/` into an id that\n   `HomeIsolation` interpolates into `home_root / eval_id / home`.\n\nBoth call sites MUST run **before any filesystem write** (NFR-SEC1\ninvariant). The unit tests in this task simulate the post-expansion\nguard call directly; the actual SuiteLoader wiring lands in T01.07 and\nits integration test verifies the no-FS-write contract via the same\n`/tmp/eval-runs` snapshot pattern that `validate_manifest` already\nhonours (`test_rejection_does_not_write_to_default_scratch_root`).\n\n## Acceptance criteria → implementation map\n\n| AC bullet (T01.05) | Implementation site |\n|---|---|\n| `validate_eval_id()` in `src/superclaude/cli/eval/loader.py` raises `InvalidEvalId` for inputs `../home`, `/etc`, `..`, empty string, leading-digit IDs, and template tokens inside id. | `tests/cli/eval/test_eval_id_regex.py::test_validate_eval_id_rejects_traversal_and_separator_patterns`, `test_validate_eval_id_rejects_empty_string`, `test_validate_eval_id_rejects_leading_digit_ids`, `test_validate_eval_id_rejects_template_token_patterns`. |\n| Guard is applied at SuiteLoader entry AND after parameterize expansion. | Unit-level surface here (`test_validate_eval_id_rejects_unsafe_expanded_id`, `test_validate_eval_id_accepts_safe_expanded_id`, `test_validate_eval_id_rejects_traversal_after_substitution`); SuiteLoader wiring + integration assertion lands in T01.07. |\n| `InvalidEvalId` propagates to process exit code 2 via the loader error mapping. | `INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE = 2` exported from the loader; asserted by `test_invalid_eval_id_exit_code_is_two`. |\n| `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/D-0005/spec.md` documents the regex and all negative cases. | This document (see \"The regex\" + \"Negative-case checklist\" sections). |\n\n## Caller contract (downstream consumers)\n\n- **COMP-002 `SuiteLoader` (T01.07)** — calls `validate_eval_id` twice\n  per eval (entry + post-expansion) as the authoritative runtime guard.\n  Maps `InvalidEvalId` to `INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE`.\n- **`HomeIsolation` (Phase 2)** — MAY re-apply the guard as a\n  belt-and-braces check immediately before `home_root / eval_id /\n  \"home\"` is constructed. This is defensive: the SuiteLoader should\n  already have rejected the id, but `HomeIsolation` is the last layer\n  before an actual FS write so the cost of the extra check (one\n  compiled regex match) is negligible relative to the failure mode.\n\n## Notes / deferred work\n\n- `EVAL_ID_REGEX` is the **runtime** copy of the FR-SCH2 regex. The\n  **schema** copy lives in `suites/suite.schema.json`. Keeping them in\n  sync is a manual contract for now; design-spec §5 explicitly calls\n  out that the runtime guard is authoritative, so a schema-vs-runtime\n  drift would be detected by the unit tests in this task.\n- The function is intentionally narrow (no logging, no allow-list, no\n  length cap). Path-containment for the scratch root is a separate\n  concern handled by AC12 (T01.19) and `HomeIsolation` (Phase 2).\n- `InvalidEvalId.eval_id` stores the original value (including non-str\n  inputs) so reporter output can render the offending payload\n  verbatim. `__str__` uses `repr()` so whitespace, empty strings, and\n  control characters survive into stderr without being silently\n  collapsed.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":208,"cache_read_input_tokens":128578,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":208,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":60,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"be850018-2718-4ed0-9c1b-850251ca2290","uuid":"44a45787-479c-406e-9d6a-72a9d7bdeb29"}

The unit tests in this task simulate the post-expansion\nguard call directly; the actual SuiteLoader wiring lands in T01.07 and\nits integration test verifies the no-FS-write contract via the same\n`/tmp/eval-runs` snapshot pattern that `validate_manifest` already\nhonours (`test_rejection_does_not_write_to_default_scratch_root`).\n\n## Acceptance criteria → implementation map\n\n| AC bullet (T01.05) | Implementation site |\n|---|---|\n| `validate_eval_id()` in `src/superclaude/cli/eval/loader.py` raises `InvalidEvalId` for inputs `../home`, `/etc`, `..`, empty string, leading-digit IDs, and template tokens inside id. | `tests/cli/eval/test_eval_id_regex.py::test_validate_eval_id_rejects_traversal_and_separator_patterns`, `test_validate_eval_id_rejects_empty_string`, `test_validate_eval_id_rejects_leading_digit_ids`, `test_validate_eval_id_rejects_template_token_patterns`. |\n| Guard is applied at SuiteLoader entry AND after parameterize expansion. | Unit-level surface here (`test_validate_eval_id_rejects_unsafe_expanded_id`, `test_validate_eval_id_accepts_safe_expanded_id`, `test_validate_eval_id_rejects_traversal_after_substitution`); SuiteLoader wiring + integration assertion lands in T01.07. |\n| `InvalidEvalId` propagates to process exit code 2 via the loader error mapping. | `INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE = 2` exported from the loader; asserted by `test_invalid_eval_id_exit_code_is_two`. |\n| `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/D-0005/spec.md` documents the regex and all negative cases. | This document (see \"The regex\" + \"Negative-case checklist\" sections). |\n\n## Caller contract (downstream consumers)\n\n- **COMP-002 `SuiteLoader` (T01.07)** — calls `validate_eval_id` twice\n  per eval (entry + post-expansion) as the authoritative runtime guard.\n  Maps `InvalidEvalId` to `INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE`.\n- **`HomeIsolation` (Phase 2)** — MAY re-apply the guard as a\n  belt-and-braces check immediately before `home_root / eval_id /\n  \"home\"` is constructed. This is defensive: the SuiteLoader should\n  already have rejected the id, but `HomeIsolation` is the last layer\n  before an actual FS write so the cost of the extra check (one\n  compiled regex match) is negligible relative to the failure mode.\n\n## Notes / deferred work\n\n- `EVAL_ID_REGEX` is the **runtime** copy of the FR-SCH2 regex. The\n  **schema** copy lives in `suites/suite.schema.json`. Keeping them in\n  sync is a manual contract for now; design-spec §5 explicitly calls\n  out that the runtime guard is authoritative, so a schema-vs-runtime\n  drift would be detected by the unit tests in this task.\n- The function is intentionally narrow (no logging, no allow-list, no\n  length cap). Path-containment for the scratch root is a separate\n  concern handled by AC12 (T01.19) and `HomeIsolation` (Phase 2).\n- `InvalidEvalId.eval_id` stores the original value (including non-str\n  inputs) so reporter output can render the offending payload\n  verbatim. `__str__` uses `repr()` so whitespace, empty strings, and\n  control characters survive into stderr without being silently\n  collapsed.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

Same numeric value (2), different\n   semantic intent. Lets call sites branch on the failure class without\n   coupling the two via a shared constant.\n\n2. **Type guard precedes regex check.** YAML can produce non-string\n   scalars (int, bool, None) for any node — even ones the schema\n   declares as `string` if the schema check is bypassed (post-expansion\n   code path constructs the id programmatically). The type guard raises\n   `InvalidEvalId` directly so non-str inputs never reach the\n   `EVAL_ID_REGEX.fullmatch` call.\n\n3. **`fullmatch`, not `match`.** Belt-and-braces: the regex is already\n   anchored (`^...$`) but `fullmatch` ignores `re.MULTILINE` quirks and\n   makes the contract intent obvious to readers.\n\n4. **No length cap.** The regex itself is a polynomial-time match on a\n   bounded character class so ReDoS is not a concern; a length cap\n   would be a separate, orthogonal AC and is not in this task's scope.\n\n5. **`InvalidEvalId.eval_id` preserves original value.** Reporter\n   output uses `repr()` rendering so whitespace, empty strings, and\n   control characters survive forensically.
